# THE COMPLETE ARCHITECTURE

**Everything, in the universal sequence. Nothing skipped.**

6 pillars · 12 steps · 27 segments · **183 containers** · **3483 rows** · 175 filters · 12 states · 7 evidence levels · 20 failure classes · 34 chain steps · 67 rubrics · 9 intent types

Human column and computer column at every node. Rubrics, filters, states, evidence levels and
failure classes placed at the step where they act, not merely listed.

---

## LAYER −1 — THE SIX MACRO PILLARS

| pillar | name | what it does | segments |
|---|---|---|---|
| **P1** | FOUNDATIONAL SUBSTRATE | Internal homeostasis — what keeps the system existing at all | `SBX-SEG-01` Biological Regulation, `SBX-SEG-02` Internal State |
| **P2** | PERCEPTION & ACTION | The environmental interface — raw world in, motor force out | `SBX-SEG-03` Perception, `SBX-SEG-04` Body Representation, `SBX-SEG-05` Sensorimotor Action, `SBX-SEG-06` Physical Execution |
| **P3** | CORE COGNITION | Ingestion and archive — filter, pattern, retain across timescales | `SBX-SEG-07` Attention, `SBX-SEG-09` Learning, `SBX-SEG-10` Memory, `SBX-SEG-11` Knowledge |
| **P4** | ADVANCED EXECUTIVE | Strategy and deliberation — simulate, solve, choose under uncertainty | `SBX-SEG-08` Executive Control, `SBX-SEG-12` Reasoning, `SBX-SEG-13` Planning, `SBX-SEG-14` Decision, `SBX-SEG-15` Creativity |
| **P5** | DRIVES, EMOTION & SELF | Agency and alignment — objective functions, priority, identity | `SBX-SEG-18` Emotion, `SBX-SEG-19` Motivation, `SBX-SEG-20` Intent, `SBX-SEG-21` Motive, `SBX-SEG-22` Consciousness, `SBX-SEG-23` Self |
| **P6** | HIGHER ORTHOGONAL | Meta and social — symbol, other minds, self-revision over time | `SBX-SEG-16` Language, `SBX-SEG-17` Communication, `SBX-SEG-24` Social Intelligence, `SBX-SEG-25` Development, `SBX-SEG-26` Metacognition, `SBX-SEG-27` Adaptation |

---

## THE SPINE — FIRST ORDER 1–8 (closes to 1) · SECOND ORDER 9–12

| # | step | order | what stands on it |
|---|---|---|---|
| **1** | **GROUND** | FIRST ORDER | The thing is already there. Nobody made it. |
| **2** | **PRESSURE** | FIRST ORDER | Something forces contact with it. |
| **3** | **USE** | FIRST ORDER | It is used long before it is understood. |
| **4** | **WITNESS** | FIRST ORDER | Someone stops using it and looks at it. Rare, and a choice. |
| **5** | **EXPRESSION** | FIRST ORDER | A way to say it is found. This — not step 1 — is the human act. |
| **6** | **NAMING** | FIRST ORDER | The name stands for the thing. THIS IS WHERE THE MASK FORMS. |
| **7** | **HALT** | FIRST ORDER | The expression fails somewhere. There is always a somewhere. |
| **8** | **LOOP** | FIRST ORDER — CLOSES TO STEP 1 | The halt becomes the next GROUND. |
| **9** | **CONSOLIDATION** | SECOND ORDER | Many cycles harden into permanent architecture. |
| **10** | **ALIENATION** | SECOND ORDER | The Mask is mistaken for Ground. The history decays. |
| **11** | **COLLISION** | SECOND ORDER — ANY-STEP EVENT | The system hits another system. The individual boundary breaks. |
| **12** | **METAMORPHOSIS** | SECOND ORDER — ANY-STEP EVENT | The framework itself breaks to build a new paradigm. |

**Step 8 closes to step 1.** Steps 9–12 are the life of the loop itself across many cycles —
11 and 12 can fire at any step, not only after 8.


========================================================================================

> *The name stands for the thing. THIS IS WHERE THE MASK FORMS.*

**FIRST ORDER**

- **HUMAN:** 'Hunger' becomes the word — and hides that there are many hungers. MASK.
- **COMPUTER:** Primary keys, strict types, frozen enums, token vocabularies — the label replaces the thing.

# STEP 7: HALT

> *The expression fails somewhere. There is always a somewhere.*

**FIRST ORDER**

- **HUMAN:** 'Hunger = need for food' fails on the other hungers. Mercury's orbit disobeys Newton.
- **COMPUTER:** NullPointer, segfault, OOM, divide-by-zero, PID saturation, out-of-vocabulary token.

**14 containers · 250 rows at this step.**

- **IT-04 OVERRIDE BY A HIGHER CLAIM — a larger obligation outranked the rule**

## LAYERS STILL OPEN — no ceiling, by his ruling

- **ARCHETYPE** — above the rows, extracted from the books. Each carries its scale axis,
  the intent types it can produce, its discriminator, and what it refuses. No count limit.
- **LINK** — relations between rows as first-class counted objects. *Diamond cut diamond*
  is a link between two ego-rows, not a row.
- **SCALE** — stored axis on every archetype. Micro · individual · relational · macro, and more.

## THE NINE INTENT TYPES — approved, placed above

- **IT-01 DISPOSITION — the act flows from what the actor IS** — reads at step 1
- **IT-02 AFFORDANCE — the moment offered it** — reads at step 2
- **IT-07 IMPATIENCE / VISIBLE PROOF — cannot hold through the long invisible process** — reads at step 2
- **IT-03 AUTOMATICITY — ran with no live reason at all** — reads at step 3
- **IT-09 PERMISSION-WAITING — stalled on a permission never asked for** — reads at step 4
- **IT-06 ROLE / VIRTUE BINDING — compelled by the actor's own standing** — reads at step 6
- **IT-04 OVERRIDE BY A HIGHER CLAIM — a larger obligation outranked the rule** — reads at step 7
- **IT-05 RECOVERY — acting to restore what is already lost** — reads at step 8
- **IT-08 FRUIT-FOCUS — acting for the reward, not the rightness of the act** — reads at step 8
